## 2026-07-16 Steam leaderboard package recovery

- BuildID `24226288` was diagnosed as a defective Steam package: `steamworks-ffi-node` was incomplete and `koffi` was absent, so the renderer selected the Cloud Global fallback and hid the existing Steam boards. The leaderboard definitions and stored scores were not deleted or changed.
- The exact known-good payload was re-uploaded to `sector-continue-test` as emergency BuildID `24226934`, depot manifest `5580543771637302882`. Independent SteamCMD `app_info_print` verification confirmed the defective build is no longer assigned; public remains unchanged on BuildID `24218172`.
- A direct probe using the repaired package's own native modules opened `nova_swarm_global_score_v2` and downloaded 20 existing entries, proving Steam initialization, native module loading, board identity, and score visibility.
- Steam packaging now stages the complete `steamworks-ffi-node` and `koffi` trees explicitly after electron-builder, then applies the hardened package-runtime gate. Smoke-only cold starts have a bounded extended load window so Windows security scanning cannot create a false package failure; normal gameplay startup is unchanged.
- A fresh packaged smoke exposed one final packaging-only path defect before upload: `steamworks-ffi-node` rewrites any `.asar` SDK path to `.asar.unpacked`, so giving it the already-unpacked staged path produced `app.asar.unpacked.unpacked` and `SteamAPI_Init()` returned false. The bridge now hands the library its expected virtual `app.asar` path after first verifying the real staged SDK exists; focused coverage locks this normalization while leaving normal development SDK paths unchanged.
- The repaired package then passed the strict Steam smoke (`ready`, native loaded, `nova_swarm_global_score_v2` available), and a live packaged read opened the real board and downloaded its top ten without submitting. The legacy control/performance harness was separately caught waiting on its renderer URL autostart and never reached gameplay; smoke-only Electron code now starts the test run explicitly and deterministically. Normal menu launch and player-facing flow are untouched.
- Final source commit `f8cfda3b1802c05f35a2262b1c2814702a96977f` was built as `v2026-07-16_02-29-16`, packaged as 417 files / 947,220,954 bytes, and uploaded only to `sector-continue-test` as BuildID `24230448`, depot manifest `1716889926300884628`. Independent SteamCMD app-info verification shows the test branch on `24230448` and public unchanged on `24218172`; the emergency known-good BuildID `24226934` remains the rollback target.
- Final verification passed release-line immediately before upload, static package runtime, strict packaged Steam smoke, current Electron smoke, packaged keyboard/gamepad controls, fresh-profile Steam isolation, desktop package review, Steam/Electron bridge contract, and 12-sample packaged performance at 55.25 minimum / 56.31 average FPS. No leaderboard definitions, stored scores, saves, achievements, Steam Cloud, store metadata, pricing, or public/default branch were changed.

## 2026-07-15 Packaged Steam runtime release gate hotfix

- Root cause: packaged smoke and desktop package review only gated scene/API/console health, so a report with `steamBridgeStatus.available=false`, `nativeModuleLoaded=false`, and `steamLeaderboardAvailable=false` could still pass and silently select the Cloud Global fallback.
- Normal packaged validation now requires the native Steam bridge and Steam leaderboard to be available. Intentional non-Steam QA remains possible only through the explicit `NOVA_SWARM_PACKAGED_SMOKE_MODE=local` opt-out, which is reported as a skipped Steam gate.
- The static package-runtime check now requires the packaged `steamworks-ffi-node` entrypoint plus its `koffi` JavaScript/native runtime, not only the standalone overlay addon.
- Focused deterministic coverage proves the defective package state fails, a ready Steam package passes, missing status fails closed, invalid modes fail closed, and the explicit local mode remains supported. No player-facing text, gameplay, score, saves, leaderboards, Steamworks settings, or deployment changed.
- Verification passed `check:packaged-steam-runtime-gate`, `check:release-line`, syntax checks, and `git diff --check`; the known-good BuildID 24222462 payload passes the hardened static gate while the defective BuildID 24226288 payload fails on its missing packaged `koffi` runtime. `check:steam-electron-bridge` independently remains red on the unchanged bridge test's expected raw-upload diagnostic (`expected SteamAPI_ISteamUserStats_UploadLeaderboardScore`, received `uploadScore`); this hotfix does not alter that bridge path.

## 2026-07-10 Autonomous 30+ Retention Goal

- Active goal: implement and verify at least 30 new player-facing improvements, perform competitor-informed design work and human-like automated playtesting, then upload one final candidate to `sector-continue-test`. No intermediate user test is required.
- Competitive direction: Brotato makes short waves and repeated build choices explicit; Deep Rock Galactic: Survivor pairs automatic combat with mission objectives; Halls of Torment foregrounds build synergy, character variety, distinct bosses, and quest direction; Nova Drift centers an evolving ship build; 20 Minutes Till Dawn emphasizes frequent upgrade choices in approachable runs. Nova Swarm already has deep ships, powerups, themes, bosses, and Pilot Orders, but much of that depth is delivered passively. The first major package therefore turns every boss clear into an active, run-only Tactical Draft choice without changing score, XP, leaderboards, achievements, or Steam metadata.
- Verified player-facing improvement ledger: (1) every boss clear now opens a Tactical Draft before the next sector; (2) combat and the run clock freeze while choosing; (3) each draft presents three distinct offers; (4) offers are deterministic for the run/sector; (5) already chosen augments stay out of the pool while unseen options remain; (6) each augment has an explicit stack cap; (7) one offense lane is guaranteed when eligible; (8) one mobility lane is guaranteed when eligible; (9) defense is prioritized at one life; (10) repair is suppressed at full lives; (11) current timed powerups receive a repetition penalty; (12) run themes weight tactically relevant categories; (13) choices persist only for the current run; (14) `DAMAGE UP` adds restrained permanent damage; (15) `RAPID FIRE` adds restrained permanent fire cadence; (16) `RAIL SURGE` improves projectile speed plus light damage; (17) `DOUBLE SHOT` adds a lane with a damage tradeoff; (18) `PIERCE` adds penetration with a damage tradeoff; (19) `TARGET PAINT` combines light damage and cadence; (20) `PLASMA LANCE` adds a heavy-fire tradeoff; (21) `CHAIN LIGHTNING` adds persistent chain reach; (22) `SPEED UP` adds permanent movement speed; (23) `BLINK DRIVE` improves movement and dodge recovery; (24) `VECTOR BOOST` improves movement and dodge duration; (25) `MAGNET` adds persistent pickup pull; (26) `DRONES` adds persistent support fire; (27) `SHIELD` restores a sector-start shield; (28) `GHOST MODE` adds sector-start safety; (29) `POINT DEFENSE` adds a sector-start interception window; (30) `BOMB` adds sector-start heavy shots; (31) `ORBITAL STRIKE` adds sector-start priority charges; (32) `NANO PATCH` repairs immediately only when useful; (33) held fire/movement must be released before keyboard/gamepad confirmation; (34) keyboard navigation and confirmation are complete; (35) controller navigation and confirmation are complete; (36) pointer hover/click are complete; (37) desktop cards have stable non-overlapping dimensions; (38) compact cards reflow vertically without overlap; (39) loaded cards use the existing generated powerup artwork; (40) early asset races use category-specific fallback emblems; (41) focus, lock-in, and confirmation feedback are visually distinct; (42) the pause menu preserves a concise tactical loadout summary; (43) Run Report records the chosen tactical build; (44) all new text is localized across all eight supported locales; (45) dodge completion no longer cancels a longer overlapping invulnerability window; (46) right-corner gameplay notices dynamically clear Lives, sector, active-powerup, and trait HUD bounds, fixing the reported `NEW THREAT SCANNED` overlap.
- Human-like runtime evidence: the final real-input starter-ship simulation held fire, steered toward active targets, dodged, cleared two complete sectors and bosses, released input at each draft, selected `PIERCE` then `RAPID FIRE`, and reached Sector 3 in `2:54.5` with score `16,898`, three lives, both augments intact, and zero browser errors. Evidence: `test-results/human-retention-run-2026-07-10T21-49-30-261Z/report.json` plus both draft screenshots and the Sector 3 live screenshot. Earlier simulations exposed the dodge/invulnerability source-clobber and held-fire auto-confirmation before either could reach a human Steam test. Both were corrected and added to regression coverage. The reported discovery/HUD collision is reproduced and proven fixed in `test-results/gameplay-message-overlap-2026-07-10T22-01-17-226Z/discovery-toast-hud-avoidance.png` with measured non-overlapping bounds.
- Final source verification: `check:tactical-draft`, `check:human-retention-run`, `check:gameplay-message-overlap`, `check:pause-context-chips`, `check:run-report`, `check:result-screen-flow`, `check:gameover-motivation`, `check:controller-flow`, `check:run-contracts`, `check:invulnerability-window-readability`, `check:phase-cooldown-readability`, `check:i18n`, `build:current`, `check:i18n-ui`, `smoke`, `desktop:smoke:current`, `check:steam-electron-bridge`, `check:gameplay-performance-analysis`, and `desktop:perf:current` pass. All eight locales have zero placeholders, English leaks, console errors, or page errors. Browser performance scenarios pass; Electron combat averaged `60.24 FPS` with `59.88 FPS` minimum and no warnings/errors. Nova Swarm is treated as a Windows PC game; compact-window checks remain for resizable PC windows, while touch/mobile-specific UX is not a product priority.

## 2026-07-10 Sector 3 Video Retention Presentation Follow-Up

- Current request: explain the nonsensical `Arcade Control reports the boss entrance - maximum pew-pew compliance` screenshot, analyze the 119.63-second Steam test recording `Desktop 2026.07.10 - 19.54.16.11.mp4`, and make a substantial retention-focused improvement package rather than another tiny adjustment. The recording starts combat around six seconds, reaches its first boss around 36 seconds, Sector 2 around 48 seconds, its second boss around 81 seconds, Sector 3 around 84 seconds, and the runback screen around 110 seconds with score `11,863` and runtime `1:39` after an intentional death.
- Video diagnosis: the screenshot is not a real boss warning. `getMicroMessage('levelStart')` assembled unrelated weighted fragments and selected `the boss entrance` during ordinary Sector 1, while the same opening also showed the returning-player Pilot Orders banner and released enemies. The recording additionally showed floating combo plates for nearly every kill, generated fake-achievement flavor inside rank/boss progression cards, and a result target of Sector 61 after a Sector 3 run because an old personal best was treated as the only useful target. The visibly travelling backdrop reads materially better in the new recording and was not changed again.
- Implementation: critical micro messages are now deterministic and factual; routine level-start/new-wave flavor toasts and the repeated returning-player Pilot Orders start banner are removed while fresh profiles retain the localized controls lesson. Returning pilots get a `1.6s` desktop / `1.4s` compact ship intro instead of `3.2s` / `2.6s`; first-run timings remain unchanged. Rank-up and boss-defeated cards no longer include generated fake-achievement filler, and boss intros show the boss archetype instead of a random phrase. Routine score popups are smaller, unframed, limited to 14 active displays, and expire in `560ms`; floating combo styling is opt-in and milestone-only, while the authoritative combo meter and score rules remain unchanged. Popup placement now checks rendered footprint and separates simultaneous near-miss/major feedback. When a historical best sector is far beyond the current run, Game Over now offers a recovery target based on the current run, so Sector 3 versus a Sector 60 record yields `Next goal: Reach sector 5`.
- Focused visual/runtime evidence: inspected clean returning-run combat, fresh-profile keyboard/controller controls, lightweight score feedback, rank-up, factual boss intro, boss defeat, and 1920x1080 distant-best result screenshots. `check:retention-presentation`, `check:score-popup-readability`, `check:run-contracts`, `check:gameover-motivation`, `check:rank-up-clarity`, `check:gameplay-message-overlap`, `check:boss-warning-popup`, `check:combo-meter-urgency`, `check:combo-flow-mechanics`, and `check:result-screen-flow` pass. Steam result submission now has bounded name/submit timeouts, a local-score fallback, queued retry, and lifecycle guards so a hung Steam promise cannot trap the result screen. Launch controls follow the device that actually started the run, so an idle connected controller cannot replace keyboard guidance. An early first-run achievement is held at game scope and delivered after onboarding or on an early Game Over instead of being lost.
- Visual evidence: fresh-profile keyboard and controller controls are fully opaque, below the HUD, and free of achievement/high-score overlap. Base, storm, and boss arena drift screenshots were inspected for nonblank rendering, edge coverage, and readable gameplay. The simplified result ceremony and immediate Steam runback screenshots were inspected at 1600x900, 1366x768, and 1280x720. ## 2026-07-08 Pilot Orders Progress Pill / Overrun Milestone Bonus Fix

- Current request: fix the main-menu Pilot Orders progress text spilling out of its pill, confirm whether inactive Pilot Orders can progress, and investigate missing bonus points after clearing sector 20.
- Diagnostic findings: Pilot Orders runtime events only iterate `session.active`, and save persistence filters back to active IDs, so non-active orders do not progress by design. A completed/queued order can be messaged as `NEXT`, which can make the next active run feel close, but it does not progress until it enters the active session. The sector-20 bonus issue was real: overrun milestone cards fired at sectors 20/30/40+, but the score award and bonus line were sector-10-only.
- Implementation direction: main-menu Pilot Orders rows now use the same measured progress slot for drawing and text fitting, with a taller centered pill so `2/5`, `1/5`, large targets, and `COMPLETE` stay inside the frame. Run-clear score bonuses now keep a per-milestone award key, preserving the sector-10 duplicate guard while allowing each overrun milestone such as sector 20 to award the same clear/spare-hulls bonus once. Overrun milestone cards now show the existing localized bonus line when those points are awarded.

## 2026-07-02 Elite Codex Destroyed Hotfix

- Current request: surgical fix for Threat Codex elite detail panels showing `Destroyed: 0` for every elite despite high encounter counts.
- Root cause: elite spawns were discovered under the `elites` Codex category, but the enemy death path queued non-boss defeats under `enemies`, so elite `timesDefeated` never reached the bucket rendered by the Elites tab.
- Implementation direction: route elite middle ship kills into the `elites` defeat bucket, include elites in the first-defeat seen-key cache, and mirror old elite defeat counts that were saved under `enemies` into `elites` during Threat Discovery normalization so existing profiles display sane counts.
- Verification direction: extend `npm run check:threat-codex` with a legacy repair regression and run focused Codex/build checks. No deploy or Steamworks change requested.

## 2026-06-26 ROW CORE Powerup And Mayhem Hype

- Current request: implement premium `ROW CORE` powerup from the attached brief and add the three low-hanging fruit ideas: more legible/hype reinforcement moments, score dopamine for surviving chaos, and more in-run boss/mission-control personality.
- Safety direction: keep all audio original/generated/static; no real stadium, football organization, team, player, song, broadcast, or fan-group references in text, filenames, or prompts. Do not put ElevenLabs keys in files or logs, and do not add runtime network calls.

- Implemented: Row Core now has a rare 7% powerup-drop branch, keeps the current weapon slot, runs six pulses over 4.2s, clears nearby bullets, pushes/damages enemies, pays +35 per bullet and +75 per enemy hit, gives +500 if already active, and grants PERFECT ROW +1000 if every pulse was useful.

## 2026-06-26 Menu Idle Boss Taunts

- Current request: menu click barks are fun, but long menu idling produced no taunts; implement the previously requested idle taunts that challenge the player to start one more run.
- Implemented: added a dedicated 30-line `boss_menu_bark_idle` voice pool, generated 30 new boss-voice MP3 files with the existing ElevenLabs menu-bark generator, and changed the menu bark manifest/catalog to derive from the bark config so per-group counts are not hardcoded.
- Runtime direction: `MenuScene` now tracks real menu inactivity from pointer, key, and controller input; after roughly 8-12 seconds idle it fires an idle boss bark, then repeats every roughly 18-30 seconds while the player keeps idling. Settings/How To Play overlays still allow idle taunts.

## 2026-06-26 Full Reinforcement Wave Retune

- Current request: boss-fight reinforcements felt too weak; boss fights should get one full reinforcement wave and sometimes two, while normal Mayhem waves should very rarely trigger a dramatic 3-4 simultaneous-wave reinforcement spike.
- Fix direction: boss reinforcements keep the existing warning and 15% boss check but now spawn full generated wave configs instead of small nerfed add groups, with an 8% double-full-wave spike from level 8 onward. Normal wave reinforcements stay at a high-action 15% event chance from level 8 onward and consume/launch 3 future waves, with a 35% chance to launch a fourth when enough future wave configs exist.

## 2026-06-26 Mayhem Super Storm

- Current request: add a 3% Mayhem super-storm event that pre-announces 2-3 seconds early, then sends 5 full wave groups at the same time, with 20 generated warning barks and 20 generated survival hero barks.
- Implementation direction: add a separate 5% normal-wave super-storm roll from level 8 onward, with first-super-storm bad-luck protection starting at level 12 and guaranteeing the first safe super storm by level 18; schedule a 2600ms boss-voice warning, spawn 5 full Mayhem reinforcement groups with 220ms spacing and offset lanes, synthesize missing wave configs when fewer than five future normal waves remain, and shout a survival bark after the current wave plus storm groups are cleared.
- Review direction: maintainer-only debug runs can force the next super storm with `Shift+M` or `window.__novaForceMayhemSuperStorm()`; the run is marked unranked before the forced review trigger.

## 2026-06-26 Reinforcement Visibility Repair

- Current request: make Mayhem reinforcements show up more reliably after a level-13 run saw none; raise the reinforcement event chance to 15% and make double reinforcements an 8% spike that adds two extra wave groups within three seconds of the warning.
- Fix direction: normal-wave and boss-fight reinforcement rolls now both use 15%; double reinforcements are allowed from level 8 onward without requiring a prior event; boss-fight checks start much earlier and can spawn two staggered reinforcement groups after the existing `INCOMING REINFORCEMENTS` warning.

## 2026-06-24 Mechanics Fairness And Readability Pass

- Current request: continue tracked feedback work by investigating fast rhombus/fast target readability, boss invulnerability wait-states, and combo flow against tanky enemies without redesigning the game or changing score formula, XP formula, leaderboard identity/behavior, achievements metadata, Steam Cloud settings, save format, Steamworks metadata, Mayhem recalibration values, Scout boss tuning, or Sector Run rules.
- Baseline safety: verified the branch includes the recent accepted Mayhem recalibration, Codex unread/Confirm Exit, UI Scale polish, How To Play/Phase Burst, Near Miss/pending leaderboard, ship unlock provenance Details display, Boss Support Ship Codex Destroyed tracking, Space Tax Audit removal, and the latest no-life-cap line before creating `snap/pre-mechanics-fairness-readability-pass-20260624` and branch `codex/mechanics-fairness-readability-pass-20260624`.
- Fast-target finding/fix: generated `fast_scout` and `fastNeedle` normal enemies were intended to be killable and already use normal Codex encounter/destroyed tracking, but several visual bodies were materially larger than their collision radius. Added a bounded readability radius floor for those fast generated targets only, capped at +3 px, without changing speed, HP, score, spawns, movement patterns, weapons, Codex IDs, bosses, Scout, Sector Run, or Mayhem recalibration.
- Boss finding: boss phase changes do not directly create invulnerability; the likely wait-state is the near-death minimum-fight finish gate, which can reject lethal damage until 9400-12600 ms and pause regular attacks. No boss behavior was changed because reducing that gate or making it active is a boss balance/design decision.
- Combo finding: score combo is kill-chain based; kills refresh the timer, damage-only hits do not, and wave cleanup can remove leftover enemies without combo kill credit. No combo/scoring change was made because hit-refresh, cleanup kill-credit, or timer changes affect score and leaderboard behavior.
- Focused evidence: `npm run check:fast-target-readability` passed with report `test-results/fast-target-readability-2026-06-24T09-22-06-859Z/report.json`; `npm run check:boss-invulnerability-audit` passed with report `test-results/boss-invulnerability-audit-2026-06-24T09-22-06-808Z/report.json`; `npm run check:combo-flow-mechanics` passed with report `test-results/combo-flow-mechanics-2026-06-24T09-22-06-817Z/report.json`.
- Steam package/upload evidence: source commit `f522114610b1ef5fed44f3f82b2509f653ab320c` packaged as `v2026-06-24_11-29-01`; packaged smoke/perf proved gitSha `f522114` at `test-results/packaged-exe-smoke-2026-06-24T09-32-53-884Z/report.json` and `test-results/packaged-perf-smoke-2026-06-24T09-32-53-992Z/report.json`; VDF kept `SetLive ""`, AppID `4765070`, and depot `4765071`; SteamCMD uploaded private unassigned BuildID `23890725`. ## 2026-06-23 Near Miss Readability And Leaderboard Polish

No gameplay/balance fix, packaging, upload, save write, Steam Cloud write, leaderboard submission, or Steamworks change is part of this pass.

- Current source tested: `f6d372a11b084550753047436432a1929591adc6` from private BuildID `23854561`. The checked-out HEAD `0dc8310ac654c8c5bdb1d0f544e59c1ce09a58dd` only adds docs/progress/release evidence on top of that source line.
- Added deterministic comparison script `scripts/analyze-mayhem-difficulty-score-delta.mjs`, using temporary detached worktrees `D:\vibe-coding-e\nova-swarm-delta-old-23809188-20260622` and `D:\vibe-coding-e\nova-swarm-delta-current-f6d372a-20260622`. It validates both worktree HEADs, verifies leaderboard identity `nova_swarm_global_score_v2`, and runs 100 seeds each for novice, medium, and high-skill/aggressive profiles.
- Latest evidence JSON: `test-results/mayhem-difficulty-score-delta-2026-06-22T14-52-53-748Z/report.json`. Docs report: `docs/nova-swarm-mayhem-difficulty-score-delta-2026-06-22.md`.
- Findings: current Mayhem is harder and less rewarding in the model. Median sector fell from 35 to 23 for the high-skill profile, median score from 422,104 to 251,856, and median XP from 20,998 to 12,754. High-skill 390k probability fell from 65% to 6%; 250k probability fell from 99% to 53%.
- Boss fairness appears directionally successful: high-skill avg boss deaths fell from 0.20 to 0.12, medium from 0.12 to 0.04, novice from 0.20 to 0.04, and modeled sectors 20-25 2+ boss-death risk fell for novice/medium profiles. High-skill boss-chain risk was already near zero in this simplified model.
- Normal waves are the likely miss: high-skill avg normal-wave deaths rose from 4.80 to 5.88 (+22.50%), while current runs reach fewer sectors and therefore collect fewer kills, waves, powerups, and late scoring opportunities. Recommendation is analysis-only: consider score/XP pacing compensation or a modest Mayhem normal-wave pressure trim after explicit approval; do not change leaderboard identity or score formula without a separate fairness decision.

- Best modeled compromise is variant F: remove the final +5% Mayhem normal-wave aggression, set `normalWaveDifficultyLevelOffset` to `7`, and apply `1.20` normal-wave score/XP compensation. - Recommendation is analysis-only pending explicit approval: F is the smallest tested variant that restores high-score chasing into the requested 250k/390k probability bands while keeping the faster Mayhem pacing. H2/H4 score higher but are larger ranked-scoring changes. Any implemented score/XP compensation would be leaderboard-affecting and should be called out before release.

## 2026-06-21 Mayhem/Sector Frame Pacing

- Current request: investigate Mayhem Run microstutter visible in `performance-video/mayhem_run.mp4` compared with smoother `sector_run.mp4`, preserve the harder ranked baseline, and deploy only after a measured source fix.
- Root cause found: Mayhem's live career-rank preview called the full game-over run summary every 300 ms during ranked gameplay, which normalized large Codex state and completion counts. Threat Codex updates also wrote the full Codex profile and requested Steam Cloud merges on every seen/defeated update.
- Fix direction: use a lightweight XP-only summary for live rank preview, keep Codex state authoritative in memory during combat, defer heavy Codex persistence while gameplay is active, and flush on run finalization/page hide. No Mayhem/Scout/Sector balance, save format, leaderboard identity, achievements metadata, Steam bridge, Steamworks settings, or profile rescue code changed.
- New evidence: `npm run check:mayhem-sector-frame-pacing` compares Mayhem Sector 1 against Sector Run checkpoint 20, records p50/p95/p99/max/long-frame counts, catches full summary calls during active play, and analyzes the ignored MP4 repro clips when present.

## 2026-06-20 Scout Codex Persistence Fix

- Current request: Scout Run must persist Threat Codex discoveries across menu return, result screen, restart/profile reload, and Steam Cloud save/load while remaining unranked and excluded from leaderboard, achievements, career XP, checkpoints, ship unlock progress, and Mayhem/ranked progress.
- Investigation direction: trace `recordThreatSeen`, `ThreatDiscoveryState`, Hangar/profile progress mirrors, renderer save collection, and Electron Steam Cloud merge/normalize to find why Scout discoveries can appear in-session but later reload to the older count.
- Root cause found: Electron Steam Cloud sanitization capped each Codex category at 500 entries, which could drop high-progress Scout-added discoveries from the profile save. The fix raises the bounded cap to current catalog scale and changes renderer/Electron Codex save merges to union/max semantics. The regression also proves profile-local Scout Best survives stale sync and restart.

## 2026-06-20 Boss Fairness Cadence Wall

- Current request: investigate boss fairness after BuildID `23836186`, especially boss 2, the sector 21 to 22 wall, and reports of bosses draining 4 or 5 lives within seconds. Do not make Mayhem or Scout globally easier.
- Investigation result: boss 2 is `Sam the Misfit`, a `forge` / `burst` / `ring` boss. Sector 21 is adjacent but not the same cadence family; sector 22 is `NOVA DEVOURER`, also `forge` / `burst` / `ring`, repeating boss 2's pattern family at much higher pressure.
- Root cause found: existing boss mercy was time-based but not encounter-aware, and respawn cleanup did not clear active boss hazard overlays or pause an already-queued boss attack. Late-sector minimum mercy could allow the same boss to chain-kill a player before real control returned.
- Fix direction: added a boss wipeout guard that tracks same-boss deaths, clears boss hazards after boss-caused death, grants stronger recovery after the second and third same-boss deaths, pauses current boss telegraphs/attack cadence briefly after respawn, and resets on boss defeat or level start. No boss HP, boss damage, mode balance, score, save, leaderboard, Steamworks, or achievement metadata changed.
- Deterministic proof: `npm run check:boss-fairness-cadence-wall` reports boss 2 first dangerous attack at 2650 ms, sector 22 sharing the Forge burst/ring cadence, pre-guard model draining 5 lives in 12 seconds, and post-guard model stopping at 3 losses with 2 lives remaining. Latest report: `test-results/boss-fairness-cadence-wall-2026-06-20T15-56-18-550Z/report.json`.
- Follow-up after private BuildID `23836473` playtest: user still lost twice to boss 2 in Mayhem and again in Scout, so the issue is not player skill; Sam's sector-2 learning check remained too punishing even after chain-death recovery. Added config-scoped `nova_boss_02` relief only for sector 2: later first danger, longer regular/signature tells, slower cadence, wider ring safe wedge, phase-2 burst reduced to 3 shots, phase-3 burst reduced to 4, and local pressure scalar `0.82`. Sector 22 remains unchanged. Latest focused report: `test-results/boss-fairness-cadence-wall-2026-06-20T16-33-05-426Z/report.json`.
- Follow-up boss cliff audit: user asked to smooth other refund-risk bosses, then clarified not to nerf bosses because bosses still need to kill players when normal waves rarely do. Added `npm run check:boss-cliff-audit` and slight scoped readability/cadence smoothing for sectors 9, 15, 18, and 19 while preserving phase 2/3 shot counts. Kept the softer sector 22 `NOVA DEVOURER` playtest version instead of hardening it back before user testing. Latest report: `test-results/boss-cliff-audit-2026-06-20T17-03-41-450Z/report.json`.

Wave fly-in work is also chunked across a few frames while preserving entry timing, removing measured wave-entry frame spikes without reducing visual quality.
- Verification evidence: `test-results/sector-arrival-spawn-delay-2026-06-16T17-57-28-740Z/` proves the stale-complete sector-2 hold, and `test-results/gameplay-performance-analysis-2026-06-16T18-03-07-563Z/` proves sector 1, sector 2, and sector 20 wave-entry p95 frame times at about 16.8ms with no >33ms frames.

## 2026-05-24 Engagement-Band Wave Spacing Follow-Up

- Current user feedback: production desktop spacing went too wide and made wave threats feel scattered. Research direction: use desktop width through readable shmup lanes, keep edges from becoming awkward traps, and adapt pressure when enemies occupy wider lanes.
- Tuned parked formations from raw desktop-span scaling to an engagement-band policy that grows by level and enemy count while keeping early/small waves in a readable central band. Wide identities such as `PINCER`, `SCREEN_DOOR`, and `CROSS_STREAM` still read wide, but no longer push ordinary level-1 waves toward both edges.
- Added per-lane pressure adaptation: outer-lane enemies get reduced fire/dive pressure and longer fire delays, while multi-elite and boss-chaos mini-boss placement now uses playable flanking lanes instead of extreme sides.

## 2026-05-24 Tight Combat Lane Correction

- Current user feedback: even the engagement-band deployment still felt far too wide in real play, especially on level 1 where a few enemies could occupy most of the desktop.
- Tightened normal-wave parked spans to a centered combat envelope: early/default waves now favor roughly one-third to two-fifths of a 1920px screen, with wide identities reserved for controlled flanking rather than full-screen parking.
- Added combat bounds to normal wave enemies so entry paths, idle sway, dives, flanking dives, and return arcs stay inside readable fight lanes. Ordinary enemies should no longer live in far-edge zip codes.

## 2026-05-21 Opening Pacing And Boss Interval Pass

- Current user request: make the first 15 minutes more exciting and better paced, increase early wave action, stop bosses from arriving every roughly 30 seconds, smooth difficulty through level 10 and beyond, and remove automatic level-clear life rewards.
- Implemented a new configurable pacing model: early sectors now run four regular waves before the boss, with level 10 sitting around five waves and later sectors scaling toward six. Early curated waves now put 29-36 enemies on screen across varied formations instead of two short waves.
- Difficulty is now a readable linear model in `BalanceConfig`: enemy health, speed, fire chance, projectile speed, boss health, and boss projectile speed scale gradually from level to level with explicit caps.
- Removed automatic life repair from normal wave clear and sector clear by moving both reward paths behind `BalanceConfig.rewards.*RepairTargetLives`, defaulting them to 0. Starting lives and intentional rare pickup/reward systems remain separate.

- Added a generated original Nova Swarm bonus-core drone asset with built-in imagegen, removed the chroma-key background locally, saved the source at `public/art/generated/nova-bonus-core-drone-source-20260517.png`, and shipped the alpha runtime sprite at `public/sprites/generated/nova-bonus-core-drone-20260517.png`.
- Updated `src/assets/assetManifest.js`, bonus drone sizing, release asset preflight, sprite catalog, and visual asset pipeline docs so menu decorations, highscore ambience, hazard drones, and collectible cores share the new original asset.
- Verification passed: `npm run build` with build ID `v2026-05-17_15-29-53`, local `npm run smoke` at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T13-30-19-186Z/`, strict 60-second `npm run playtest:release` at `test-results/release-playtest-bonus-core-20260517-1530/`, and `npm run audit:release-readiness` with only known manual blockers.
- Deployed the verified build to Cloudflare Pages production at `https://c66a8f98.burt-game.pages.dev`; `https://burt.tinyfoundry.app/version.json` reports `v2026-05-17_15-29-53`, the generated sprite returns `200 image/png`, and live-domain smoke passed at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T13-36-30-834Z/`.
- Added a compact active-powerup HUD meter with a colored duration bar, fixed its right-side layout so it sits below the sector label, and removed a stale debug-only `BEER PICKUP` toast.
- Focused HUD verification passed at `test-results/manual-powerup-hud-20260517-1553/powerup-hud.png`, `npm run build` passed with build ID `v2026-05-17_15-53-16`, full local `npm run smoke` passed at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T13-55-24-048Z/`, live-domain smoke passed at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T13-59-45-653Z/` after deploying `https://cc7b0b42.burt-game.pages.dev`, and `npm run audit:release-readiness` still reports only known manual blockers.
- Remaining blockers are still Steam-specific/manual: real Steamworks app/depot IDs, SteamPipe upload and Steam-client install/launch validation, final human approvals for screenshots/capsules/trailer/audio/store copy/legal/gameplay feel, and final email only after those are genuinely complete.

## 2026-05-17 Steam Store Metadata Handoff

- Added `release/steamworks/store_metadata_draft.json` with structured Steam page copy, tags, categories, launch option, platform, system requirements, upload-asset pointers, and explicit v1 decisions to defer Steam Cloud and Steam achievements.
- Added `docs/steam-store-handoff.md` and `scripts/check-steam-store-metadata.mjs`; package script `npm run check:steam-store` validates the draft and writes `release/steamworks/store_metadata_review_report.json`.

- Verification: `npm run check:steam-store` passed with 15 tags, 8 feature bullets, `Partial Controller Support`, and no warnings; `npm run audit:release-readiness` now reports 11 passing checks and only the known Steam/manual blockers.
- Added repeatable smoke coverage for the active powerup HUD meter. The smoke harness now captures `02-powerup-hud.png`, asserts the `RAPID FIRE` label, checks bounds, and blocks if the badge overlaps the sector label; latest local smoke passed at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T14-16-04-304Z/`.

## 2026-05-17 Asset Provenance Gate

- Added `release/provenance/asset_provenance_manifest.json`, `docs/asset-provenance.md`, and `scripts/check-asset-provenance.mjs` so shipped public/release assets must be covered by a provenance rule before Steam-readiness audit passes.

- Wired `check:provenance` into `npm run verify:steam-rc` and `npm run audit:release-readiness`; latest fast RC pass is `test-results/steam-rc-verify-2026-05-17T14-27-56-483Z/`, with only Steamworks IDs, Steam client validation, and human approvals remaining as blockers.

## 2026-05-17 Nova Swarm Retheme

- Pivoted the game away from the internal-joke identity and toward Nova Swarm, a public arcade shooter built around swarm formations, bonus cores, boss patterns, and high-score humor.
- Removed shipped real-person/private-joke images from `public/` and disabled legacy lore-photo loading in favor of generated comms portraits.
- Rewrote the README, manifest copy, store-readiness draft copy, visible menu/gameplay/boss/powerup/rank/ship text, and arcade phrase pool for the new public-facing tone.
- Replaced visible beer-can/personal-joke labels with bonus core, bonus drone, and arcade-comedy language while leaving a few internal compatibility identifiers in place where changing them would risk wave cleanup or saved local state.
- Confirmed the local imagegen skill still lacks `OPENAI_API_KEY`; no new live imagegen assets were produced in this pass.
- Latest verified checks in this pass: `npm run build`, `npm run check:audio`, and `npm run smoke` all pass. Latest smoke report: `test-results/smoke-2026-05-17T07-44-21-859Z/`.

## 2026-05-16

- Started from `main` at `4b3c598` from `origin/main`.

- Initial `npm install` succeeded.

- Restored tracked root `index.html` from git history and stopped prebuild from mutating tracked HTML on every build.
- `npm run build` now succeeds. Remaining build warnings: one mixed static/dynamic import around `ShipMetadata.js`, and a >500 kB app chunk.
- Baseline smoke screenshots showed a playable but prototype-feeling menu and a few early-session regressions: missing default ship on autostart, missing favicon, a huge early photo flyby, Pixi v8 text/display warnings, and a caught intro overlay null update.
- Generated original arctic/Northern Norway key art with the local image generation flow, saved the source in `public/art/generated/`, and added an optimized WebP menu backdrop.
- Upgraded the menu presentation with the new generated backdrop, toned-down beer-can decoration, cleaner title/subtitle copy, and subtler idle motion.
- Reused the generated arctic art as a dimmed gameplay backdrop under the parallax starfield so play now visually matches the upgraded menu while keeping bullets/enemies readable.
- Fixed the default ship path for autostart, hardened ship usage tracking, added missing door SFX manifest entries, fixed a missing `life_up` fallback, added a favicon link, and prevented dev-mode build-ID reload loops.
- Stabilized the ship intro and delayed/tamed early easter-egg photo flybys so the first seconds of play are readable.
- Migrated high-traffic Pixi text surfaces to a shared v8-safe `createText` helper across gameplay, HUD-adjacent overlays, score popups, boss text, ship screens, highscores, and game over.
- Added `npm run smoke`, a repeatable Playwright/system-Chrome production smoke test that starts Vite preview, captures menu/gameplay screenshots, records console/page/HTTP failures, and saves a JSON report under ignored `test-results/`.
- Added a real pause overlay with resume and quit-to-menu actions, and hardened input so short pause key taps cannot be missed between frames.
- Hardened PlayScene replay/scene reuse by recreating the input manager after destroy instead of leaving the next run with dead listeners.
- Split production bundles into app, Pixi vendor, and remaining vendor chunks so the main app chunk dropped from about 643 kB to about 352 kB while keeping the build warning-free.
- Added a real settings overlay from both the main menu and pause menu with music/voice toggles, master/music/SFX/voice volume sliders, and fullscreen.
- Fixed default audio preference loading so missing localStorage values keep sane defaults instead of becoming 0%.

- Extended `npm run smoke` to verify the settings overlay and include parsed text-state snapshots in its JSON report.

Font provenance is documented under `public/fonts/`, `docs/visual-asset-pipeline.md`, and `release/provenance/asset_provenance_manifest.json`, and `scripts/check-asset-provenance.mjs` now scans font/license assets. Verification passed with `node --check src/scenes/MenuScene.js`, `node --check scripts/check-asset-provenance.mjs`, `npm run check:provenance` (1627/1627 assets), `npm run build:current`, focused local menu click checks and screenshot `test-results/menu-redesign-local-1920x955-v3.png`, local smoke `test-results/smoke-2026-05-18T07-28-47-028Z/`, Cloudflare deployment `https://efc09e80.burt-game.pages.dev`, focused private-domain menu click checks and screenshot `test-results/menu-redesign-live-1920x955.png`, live font URL checks, live smoke `test-results/smoke-live-menu-redesign-2026-05-18T09-30/`, and `npm run check:live-deployment`. `https://burt.tinyfoundry.app/version.json` reports `v2026-05-18_09-30-30`.
- Fixed Steam capture tooling after the boot-flow change: screenshot and trailer capture now verify the game starts on the main menu, then deliberately open the optional Story Intro from the menu before capturing story beats. Refreshed current Steam screenshots under `release/steam-screenshots/draft-2026-05-17-current/` and refreshed trailer visual/audio/candidate evidence under `release/steam-trailer/draft-2026-05-17-current/` and `release/steam-trailer/candidate-2026-05-17-current/`; reports are current-build and clean with zero console/page/network failures.
- Corrected an early-game pacing regression found by the full RC gate: level 4's curated wave script now uses three snappier arcade waves instead of four high-evasion/high-stall waves, preserving the level-4 boss route while keeping the first 10 minutes from bogging down. Focused release playtest then survived 599,919 ms, reached level 6, and reported zero console/page/network failures; the final full Steam RC verification also passed with a 599,947 ms release playtest reaching level 6 and ending alive in the level-6 boss fight.
- Published the latest built `dist` directly to Cloudflare Pages without changing the current build ID, so `https://burt.tinyfoundry.app` now has the optional-intro capture flow and level-4 pacing fix. Live private-domain smoke passed at `test-results/smoke-2026-05-18T11-20-35-052Z/`, `npm run check:live-deployment` passed, `npm run check:full-rc` passed, and the refreshed human-review, Steam-client preflight, and release-handoff packets are current. Final `npm run audit:release-readiness` passes all automated gates and correctly leaves only the manual blockers: Steamworks app/depot IDs, real Steam-client validation evidence, and human release approvals.
- Added a guarded Steam-client validation writer, `npm run steamworks:write-client-validation`, so the final `release/steamworks/client_validation_report.json` can be generated consistently after a real SteamPipe upload and Steam-client test. The helper refuses to write unless the operator supplies the Steam build ID, validator name, existing Steam install path, existing screenshot evidence, and the explicit `STEAM_CLIENT_VALIDATION_CONFIRM=I_REVIEWED_STEAM_CLIENT_BUILD` plus `STEAM_CLIENT_ALL_CHECKS_PASSED=YES` confirmations. Updated the preflight packet, handoff packet, client validation template, and runbook to document the optional-intro validation wording and the guarded command. Verification passed with syntax checks, an expected refusal run without confirmation env vars, regenerated `npm run steamworks:client-preflight`, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and `npm run audit:release-readiness`, which still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Added a guarded human release approval writer, `npm run steamworks:write-human-approval`, so final approval can be recorded consistently without hand-editing the gate file. The helper refuses to write unless the reviewer supplies `HUMAN_RELEASE_APPROVAL_CONFIRM=I_REVIEWED_NOVA_SWARM_RELEASE_CANDIDATE`, `HUMAN_RELEASE_ALL_GATES_APPROVED=YES`, and a real reviewer name, and it checks the required screenshot, capsule, trailer, audio, store, provenance, and full-RC evidence files exist before marking all approval gates. Updated the human-review packet, approval file instructions, and release handoff packet to point at the guarded command. Verification passed with syntax checks, an expected refusal run without confirmation env vars, regenerated `npm run steamworks:human-review`, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and `npm run audit:release-readiness`, which still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Hardened the private/internal-material release gate to scan the built `dist/` bundle as well as source, docs, public assets, functions, scripts, and release metadata. `npm run audit:release-readiness` now includes `no_forbidden_private_terms_in_built_dist`, requiring the current build output to exist and contain no blocked private terms in text files or filenames. Verification passed with `node --check scripts/audit-release-readiness.mjs`, `npm run audit:release-readiness`, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and a final release audit that still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Hardened the Steamworks ID release gate so `steamworks_ids_configured` now validates the generated local VDF contents instead of only checking for `release/steamworks/app_build_LOCAL.vdf`. The audit now requires non-zero numeric app/depot IDs, no placeholder IDs, `ContentRoot` pointed at `..\\desktop\\win-unpacked`, recursive `LocalPath "*"`, `DepotPath "."`, and the packaged `Nova Swarm.exe` under that content root. Verification passed with `node --check scripts/audit-release-readiness.mjs`, a temp-output `npm run steamworks:write-vdf`, a guarded default-path fake numeric VDF rehearsal where the audit recognized `steamworks_ids_configured`, immediate removal of the ignored local VDF, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and a final `npm run audit:release-readiness` that returned to the real blockers: Steamworks IDs, Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Hardened the final manual evidence validators so approvals and Steam-client validation cannot pass as stale paperwork. `npm run steamworks:write-client-validation` now records the current `public/version.json` build in `gameBuild`, the client validation template includes that field, and `npm run audit:release-readiness` now requires a matching current game build, real validator name/date, existing Steam install path, existing screenshot evidence, all required Steam-client checks, and a Steam build ID. The human approval audit now also requires the approved `build:` line to match the current build. Verification passed with syntax checks, an expected no-env refusal, a temp confirmed client-validation rehearsal proving `gameBuild.version` matches the current build, regenerated `npm run steamworks:client-preflight`, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and a final audit that still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Added a Steam payload checksum manifest gate for the standalone Windows build. `npm run steamworks:payload-manifest` now writes `release/steamworks/steam_payload_manifest.json` with per-file SHA-256 hashes, total bytes, executable metadata, and current build metadata for `release/desktop/win-unpacked`; the release audit rehashes the payload and rejects missing, stale, changed, or unlisted files. The Steam-client preflight and release handoff packets now include the payload manifest. Verification passed with syntax checks, `npm run steamworks:payload-manifest` (74 files, 601,470,445 bytes), regenerated `npm run steamworks:client-preflight`, regenerated `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and `npm run audit:release-readiness`, which still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, real Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.
- Fixed the intro voice overlap risk reported from the live build. `AudioManager` now tracks active voice instances, exposes active voice telemetry through `render_game_to_text`, and can stop all active voices before exclusive story narration starts, advances, finishes, or is destroyed. `IntroScene` now uses that exclusive path so mission-control/settings voice auditions cannot overlap the narrator. Added `npm run check:intro-voice`, which deliberately starts a mission-control voice, opens the optional story intro, rapidly advances a panel, and verifies exactly one active narrator voice on each intro panel. Verification passed with syntax checks, `npm run build:current`, `npm run check:intro-voice`, `npm run check:audio`, `npm run smoke`, `npm run audit:audio-mix`, full `npm run verify:steam-rc -- --full` at `test-results/steam-rc-verify-2026-05-18T12-21-03-738Z/report.json` including a 599,980 ms release playtest reaching level 6, regenerated `npm run check:full-rc`, `npm run steamworks:payload-manifest` (74 files, 601,471,630 bytes), `npm run steamworks:human-review`, `npm run steamworks:client-preflight`, `npm run steamworks:handoff`, and final `npm run audit:release-readiness`, which still correctly blocks only on Steamworks IDs, real Steam-client validation evidence, and human approvals.

- Built a repeatable Nova Swarm release QA gauntlet on branch `ralph/steam-game-polish-20260520` after pushing backup branch `backup/pre-release-qa-gauntlet-20260522-1938` at `ff76c4c2c28bccb78acfd3fdf0d0d956c52aca78`. Added `npm run qa:release` plus focused QA aliases for progression, leaderboard, enemy variety, audio, and UI flow. Fixed QA-tooling drift in the audio mix audit so the 50 One More Run CTA voice clips are measured, and fixed `check:gameover-ceremony` so its live cue scores account for the new 0.1 score scale. Verification so far passed roster/enemy/boss/progression/leaderboard/audio/build/smoke gates, with release report evidence in `docs/RELEASE_QA_GAUNTLET.md`. Main findings: the default 10-minute release playtest ended by normal game over at 498.764s, peak level 5, with zero technical failures; fastest/highest-damage selected-ship probes were technically stable but did not clear wave 1 under automation; six boss/tractor SFX raw-peak at 0.0 dB; and game-over copy can confusingly mix this-run level with career-best next-goal messaging.
- Completed the first-boss regression follow-up after pushing backup branch `backup/pre-boss-regression-followup-20260522-2230` at `607b85c3be86fefe5e1eaca131d9786e23e2c132`. Root cause was stacked early friction: six-plus normal waves before boss 1, early harsh wave tactics, first-boss aimed cone/fan hazard damage layered over the shot pattern, active-combat boss dossier clutter, and a release bot that did not read boss hazards. Restored focused two-wave early boss access, delayed harsh wave tactics, made boss-1 aimed hazards non-damaging while keeping shots, changed active boss notices to compact toasts, added `npm run check:first-boss-balance`, improved release-playtest hazard/bottom-lane behavior, clarified game-over this-run versus career copy, and reduced six boss/tractor SFX files by 2 dB. Verification passed `npm run check:first-boss-balance` (lives 3->4, 25s, no damage), `npm run playtest:release` (599.926s, level 13, 1 life, no console/page/network failures), selected fastest/highest-damage 2-minute probes (both reached level 4), `npm run smoke`, `npm run qa:release`, boss checks, enemy variety/wave checks, `npm run build:current`, and `npm run audit:audio-mix` with no warnings. Vite chunk warning remains low severity at about 712.74 kB.

The Steamworks English short description was successfully changed via the normal Localization tab JSON upload flow and verified from a fresh Steamworks tab. Screenshot upload/reorder remains pending because Steamworks did not accept the new files through visible drag/drop or file-chooser automation; the support URL was not changed and `https://novaswarm.tinyfoundry.app` was not used.

- Increased the max-life economy on 2026-05-25 from 5 to 6 via shared balance config. Reaching 6 lives now triggers a localized on-screen `MAX LIVES REACHED!` notification, a procedural green-white ring burst around the player, and a new ElevenLabs Mission Control line (`mission_control_lives_max.mp3`) registered in the voice manifest/catalog/checks. Steamworks and deploy were untouched.
- Added boss mercy and clearer ship trait explanations on 2026-05-25. Boss-caused contact, boss-owned bullets, and boss hazards now share a tunable recovery window (`7000ms` early, `5750ms` at level 6, `5000ms` at level 10, minimum `2500ms`) with contact separation so a boss cannot chain all lives while the player is recovering. Hangar details now explain real trait mechanics such as SIGNAL PING wing shots, shot-based counters, visible effects, and tradeoffs; the HUD now says counters are in shots. Added `npm run check:boss-mercy` and `npm run check:ship-trait-explanations`.
- Follow-up gameplay polish on 2026-05-25 fixed Bomb powerup visibility by detonating bomb shots on impact as well as at their apex, added a short non-attacking boss finish hold so overpowered runs cannot delete a boss presentation instantly, made tractor debuffs and the tractor pull more obvious, reduced tractor elite selection weight while lifting other early elite weights, pulled elite/formation positions down from the top HUD band, and changed max-life extra-life pickups to show the localized `MAX LIVES REACHED!` notice. Added `npm run check:gameplay-followups`; verification passed focused gameplay checks, `npm run build:current`, `npm run check:i18n-ui`, `npm run check:ship-unlock-reveal`, `npm run check:first-boss-balance`, and `npm run smoke`.
- Made sectors meaningful on 2026-05-25 by replacing rotating flavor locations with deterministic one-sector-per-level progression labels. Level intros and the HUD now use the same non-repeating sector catalog (`SECTOR 19: Rook Vey`, etc.), the current sector is exposed in `render_game_to_text`, and `npm run check:sector-progression` verifies 300 unique labels plus the HUD/source wiring. Added `npm run reset:local-unlocks` to reset ship unlock progress, selected ship, local/cloud achievement mirrors, and Steam backend achievements for app `4765070` when Steam is available; the live reset cleared the 9 previously unlocked rank achievements and verified `steamAfter: []`.
- Changed sector 10 on 2026-05-26 from a hard run stop into the official clear milestone that continues into overrun. Clearing the sector-10 boss now marks the run cleared once, awards the clear/spare-hulls bonus, stores clear-time lives for XP/milestone unlocks, shows the localized `RUN CLEAR! OVERRUN UNLOCKED` toast, and advances to sector 11+ instead of ending the run. ## 2026-07-13 Ace/Nemesis reward pickup deduplication

- Player screenshot feedback exposed overlapping reward pickups after an Ace kill. Root cause: the Ace bounty and its paired Nemesis Protocol each spawned a physical reward, with only a 24-pixel horizontal offset; matching reward types therefore appeared as a duplicated powerup.

## 2026-07-14 - Galaga-informed Cabinet Skill Flights

- Studied Bandai Namco's Galaga designer interview, Taito's Space Invaders Extreme rules, the PlayStation introduction to Resogun, and Housemarque's Nex Machina design pitch. The actionable pattern was a short rules change that tests mastery, makes an earlier power gain newly valuable, grades execution, and then returns immediately to the main combat rhythm.
- Rebuilt the old fixed-bonus `Bonus Drone Raid` as `Cabinet Skill Flight`. Five deterministic choreographies now send one-hit targets through a safe combat band: Star Parade, Crosscut, Needle Dance, Orbit Waltz, and Pincer Polka. Targets have animated gold/cyan reticles, never shoot, cannot damage the player on contact, escape after a bounded window, and cannot inherit Ace, Rival Wing, rare-visitor, elite, or Codex-discovery interruptions. The rescued companion ship's double firepower now has a natural precision-stage payoff.
- Added a live lower-left flight panel with pattern, localized target count, countdown, and progress rail. Completion grades PERFECT/A/B/C/MISS from actual hits, grants a bounded score bonus through the existing global score policy, and uses the existing premium wave-clear audiovisual language. PERFECT adds the larger result card, gold/white bursts, and controlled impact; pending rank feedback is deferred so the two celebrations never compete.
- Preserved leaderboard integrity: the stage adds no hostile bullets or collision risk, so it cannot award a free flawless-wave bonus or advance the flawless streak. The normal global score normalization, 15% tuning, run-mode compensation, score breakdown, and leaderboard identities remain unchanged.
- Added complete dedicated copy for German, Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, Russian, Simplified Chinese, Korean, and Japanese. `render_game_to_text` exposes live pattern, targets, kills, escapes, time, and last result. The focused contract is part of `build:current`, and installed-Chrome runtime verifies eight harmless/unarmed targets, no bullets, no distracting Codex panel, contact safety, PERFECT, a four-of-eight grade B, score accounting, presentation focus, desktop containment, and German 840x640 containment.

## 2026-07-17 - Severe frame-pacing rescue

- Started from exact rejected-candidate source `426e94490e79be32c37863fa57a69fcbe8685f41` in clean worktree `D:\vibe-coding-e\nova-swarm-frame-pacing-rescue-20260717` on branch `codex/frame-pacing-rescue-20260717`. The dirty original worktree remains untouched.
- Measured both supplied 1920x1080 60 fps clips. Roughly 69% of encoded frames repeat; effective fresh output is 18-19 fps; and the dominant pattern is one new frame followed by two repeats. This is a presentation/capture regression, not a 60 fps encoding-metadata issue.
- Isolated the first causative runtime change to the persistent `steamworks-ffi-node` Electron capture helper introduced after public-good source `f116b716`. It serially calls full-window `capturePage()`, converts to a CPU bitmap, uploads to a native overlay, then waits another 16 ms. Browser and ordinary Electron performance checks remain near 60 fps because those modes explicitly skip this helper.
- Replaced the persistent full-frame mirror with an F12-only one-shot Electron capture that adds a PNG to the Steam screenshot library. Added a video cadence analyzer that emits per-frame timestamps, hashes, change/duplicate state, fresh-frame interval percentiles, duplicate-run histograms, and a direct one-new-plus-two-repeated detector.
- The steady 1920x1080 Electron diagnostic scene passes at 59.81 rAF Hz, 59.91 render/canvas submissions Hz, and 59.71 fixed-step Hz; p50/p95/p99/max are 16.7/16.9/17.2/17.4 ms with no intervals above 25 ms. Representative Mayhem and Sector gameplay both hold 60.0 average FPS with 16.9 ms p95, 17.2 ms p99, no active-wave interval above 20 ms, and no interval above 50 ms.
- Source-Electron live Steam screenshot integration passes with a valid 1920x1080 image at entropy 6.57 while `continuousMirror` is false. Browser cadence, i18n for all eight locales, UI localization, browser smoke, Electron smoke, controller-only flow, Steam bridge contract, release-line guard, and targeted gameplay pacing checks pass.
- Steamworks settings, public/default, and deploy remain untouched. Private packaging/upload and final Steam Game Recording fresh-frame verification are still pending.

## 2026-07-19 - No Repair Receipts investigation

- Follow-up prompt: investigate and fix `No Repair Receipts` after a player cleared Sector 10 without losing a life, continued well into Overrun, and finished above 250,000 points without receiving the achievement.
- Started clean at stable evidence baseline `ae1d2e82accf20859da172f636907a11c965cf3d` in `C:\tmp\nova-swarm-post-stable-development-20260718` on `codex/post-stable-development-20260718`; the locked stable worktree and both release tags remain untouched.
- Root cause: Sector 10 calls `Game.markRunClear()` and snapshots remaining lives, but not life losses. Milestone evaluation happens only when the run eventually ends and `No Repair Receipts` checks the final `lifeLosses`, so an Overrun death retroactively invalidates a legitimate clean Sector 10 clear.
- Added a regression case requiring a clean Sector 10 snapshot to remain eligible after a later Overrun life loss, while still rejecting a life lost before the clear. The baseline failed this exact case.
- Fixed the achievement by snapshotting `runClearLifeLosses` when Sector 10 marks the run clear, preserving it as `clearLifeLosses` in the final summary and Run Report, and evaluating `ACH_NO_REPAIR_RECEIPTS` against that clear-time snapshot. Final total `lifeLosses` remains unchanged and still includes later Overrun losses.
- Verified the logged-in local/Steam profile read-only before remediation: best score `311,495`, best sector `60`, 60 run clears, 59/81 achievements, and `ACH_NO_REPAIR_RECEIPTS` locked locally and on Steam with no pending sync queue. Steam achievement connectivity and `RequestCurrentStats` were healthy.
- Awarded only `ACH_NO_REPAIR_RECEIPTS` through the normal game achievement manager using a support backfill payload. Local persistence succeeded, Steam reported `unlocked: true`, and the pending Steam queue remained empty. Visible Steam-client confirmation showed `No Repair Receipts` in today's post-game summary and 60/81 achievements.
- Focused checks pass: milestone achievements, achievement catalog, Steam achievement mock, Steam/Electron bridge, Overrun clear static guard, real-browser Overrun clear score runtime, Run Report, release-line, i18n, and `build:current`. The repository-native Game Over screenshot was inspected and remained correct. The required generic web-game client ran after provisioning its exact browser; its state snapshots were healthy, while its direct WebGL canvas screenshots reproduced the known black-canvas capture limitation.

## 2026-07-19 - Bomb launch direction fix

- Follow-up prompt: fix the Bomb that visibly falls below the player instead of reaching enemies above, then deploy the verified correction to Steam.
- Continued from clean No Repair Receipts upload evidence commit `8fb58eaf7784dbc2685f01f4296bfc7e6ae88f42` on isolated branch `codex/bomb-direction-fix-20260719`. The locked stable worktree and release tags remain untouched.
- Confirmed the mismatch: Bomb targeting accepts only valid enemies above the player, but `Player.shoot()` gave the Bomb positive vertical velocity. Nova Swarm uses a top-left origin with positive Y downward, so the projectile moved away from every target it had just locked.
- Replaced the old test shortcut that teleported an enemy onto the newly created Bomb. The focused runtime now requires negative/upward velocity, advances the real projectile frame by frame, and proves it reaches the enemy, detonates, kills, awards score, cleans visuals, and consumes the expected charge.

## 2026-07-25 - Boss-warning steering continuity
